<!DOCTYPE html><html lang="en"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1"><script>
			(() => {
				const KEY = "ui-mode";
				const media = window.matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: dark)");
				const isPref = (v) => v === "light" || v === "dark" || v === "auto";

				const readPref = () => {
					try {
						const v = localStorage.getItem(KEY);
						return isPref(v) ? v : "auto";
					} catch {
						return "auto";
					}
				};

				const resolveMode = (pref) =>
					pref === "light" || pref === "dark"
						? pref
						: media.matches
							? "dark"
							: "light";

				const applyTheme = () => {
					const root = document.documentElement;
					const pref = readPref();
					const mode = resolveMode(pref);
					// data-mode drives Nimbus dark-mode CSS; data-theme is kept for
					// any ported component CSS that still keys off Starlight's attribute.
					if (mode === "dark") root.setAttribute("data-mode", "dark");
					else root.removeAttribute("data-mode");
					root.setAttribute("data-theme", mode);
					root.style.colorScheme = mode;
					// Drive the toggle icon from the root attribute (set here, pre-paint)
					// so it never flashes the SSR default before the toggle script loads.
					root.setAttribute("data-nb-pref", pref); // selected preference
					root.setAttribute("data-nb-state", mode); // resolved (light/dark)
				};

				applyTheme();
				document.addEventListener("astro:after-swap", applyTheme);
				// Live OS changes only move the page when the user is following the
				// system (auto). An explicit light/dark choice is left untouched.
				media.addEventListener("change", () => {
					if (readPref() === "auto") applyTheme();
				});

				// Cross-tab sync.
				window.addEventListener("storage", (e) => {
					if (e.key === KEY) applyTheme();
				});

				window.__nbApplyTheme = applyTheme;
			})();
		</script><style>

				transition: none !important;

				display: none;

				[data-nb-cli-variant="wrangler"] {
				display: none;
			}

			html[data-nb-cli-preference="cf"]
				[data-nb-cli-preference-scope]
				[data-nb-cli-variant="cf"] {
				display: block;
			}
		</style><script>
	(() => {
		const root = document.documentElement;
		const isPreference = (value) => value === "wrangler" || value === "cf";
		const readStoredPreference = () => {
			try {
				const stored = localStorage.getItem("ui-cli-preference");
				return isPreference(stored) ? stored : undefined;
			} catch {
				return undefined;
			}
		};
		const readDefault = (pathname) =>
			pathname === "/cf" || pathname.startsWith("/cf/") ? "cf" : "wrangler";
		const resolvePreference = (pathname) => {
			if (isPreference(window.__nbCliPreferenceOverride)) {
				return window.__nbCliPreferenceOverride;
			}
			const stored = readStoredPreference();
			if (stored) return stored;
			return readDefault(pathname);
		};

		if (!root.dataset.nbCliPreference) {
			root.dataset.nbCliPreference = resolvePreference(location.pathname);
		}

		if (!window.__nbCliPreferenceBound) {
			document.addEventListener("astro:before-swap", (event) => {
				const newRoot = event.newDocument.documentElement;
				newRoot.dataset.nbCliPreference = resolvePreference(event.to.pathname);
			});
			window.__nbCliPreferenceBound = true;
		}
	})();

âï¸</span></a></li></ul></div></div></div></li></ul></div><script type="module" src="/_astro/Sidebar.astro_astro_type_script_index_0_lang.C5NaZfBO.js"></script></div></nav></div><div class="from-base pointer-events-none sticky bottom-0 h-8 bg-gradient-to-t to-transparent"></div></aside><script>
	(function () {
		try {
			if (!matchMedia("(min-width: 64rem)").matches) return;
			const sidebar = document.getElementById("desktop-sidebar");
			if (!sidebar) return;
			const content = sidebar.querySelector("[data-nb-sidebar]");
			if (!content) return;

			function setGroupOpen(group, open) {
				group.setAttribute("data-nb-default-open", open ? "true" : "false");
				const trigger = group.querySelector("[data-nb-collapsible-trigger]");
				const panel = group.querySelector("[data-nb-collapsible-content]");
				const state = open ? "open" : "closed";
				if (trigger) {
					trigger.setAttribute("data-nb-state", state);
					trigger.setAttribute("aria-expanded", String(open));
				}
				if (panel) panel.setAttribute("data-nb-state", state);
			}

			const raw = sessionStorage.getItem("sidebar-state");
			let state;
			if (raw) {
				state = JSON.parse(raw);
				if (state && content.dataset.nbSidebarHash === state.hash) {
					const groups = content.querySelectorAll("[data-nb-sidebar-group]");
					for (let i = 0; i < groups.length; i++) {
						if (typeof state.open[i] === "boolean") {
							setGroupOpen(groups[i], state.open[i]);
						}
					}
				}
			}

			// Active page's group must always be open, regardless of stored state
			const active = sidebar.querySelector("[aria-current='page']");
			if (active) {
				let d = active.closest("[data-nb-sidebar-group]");
				while (d) {
					setGroupOpen(d, true);
					d =
						d.parentElement &&
						d.parentElement.closest("[data-nb-sidebar-group]");
				}
			}

			// Restore scroll or center active item
			if (raw && state && state.scroll) {
				sidebar.scrollTop = state.scroll;
			} else if (active) {
				const sr = sidebar.getBoundingClientRect();
				const ar = active.getBoundingClientRect();
				sidebar.scrollTop += ar.top - sr.top - sr.height / 2 + ar.height / 2;
			}
		} catch {
			// Never let state restore break navigation.
		}
	})();
</script><div class="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col"><div class="flex min-w-0 flex-1"><main id="main-content" class="min-w-0 flex-1"><div class="mx-auto px-4 pt-6 pb-12 lg:px-6 class="docs-content max-w-none">
<p>By default, users have access to the Workers Free plan. The Workers Free plan includes limited usage of Workers, Pages Functions, Workers KV and Hyperdrive. Read more about the <a href="/workers/platform/limits/#account-plan-limits">Free plan limits</a>.</p>
<p>The Workers Paid plan includes Workers, Pages Functions, Workers KV, Hyperdrive, and Durable Objects usage for a minimum charge of $5 USD per month for an account. The plan includes increased initial usage allotments, with clear charges for usage that exceeds the base plan. There are no additional charges for data transfer (egress) or throughput (bandwidth).</p>
<p>All included usage is on a monthly basis.</p>
<aside role="note" aria-label="Pages Functions billing" class="aside-card flex items-start gap-3 rounded-lg px-4 py-3 my-4" style="--_c: var(--nb-info); --_t: var(--nb-info-muted);" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><span class="flex h-[1.375em] shrink-0 items-center" aria-hidden="true" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><svg width="1em" height="1em" viewBox="0 0 256 256" class="h-[1em] w-[1em]" data-astro-cid-znle5jil="true" data-icon="ph:info"><path fill="currentColor" d="M128 24a104 104 0 1 0 104 104A104.11 104.11 0 0 0 128 24m0 192a88 88 0 1 1 88-88a88.1 88.1 0 0 1-88 88m16-40a8 8 0 0 1-8 8a16 16 0 0 1-16-16v-40a8 8 0 0 1 0-16a16 16 0 0 1 16 16v40a8 8 0 0 1 8 8m-32-92a12 12 0 1 1 12 12a12 12 0 0 1-12-12"/></svg></span><div class="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col gap-0.5" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><p class="m-0 text-base leading-snug font-semibold" data-astro-cid-znle5jil>Pages Functions billing</p><div class="aside-card-body text-sm leading-normal" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><p>All <a href="/pages/functions/">Pages Functions</a> are billed as Workers. All pricing and inclusions in this document apply to Pages Functions. Refer to <a href="/pages/functions/pricing/">Functions Pricing</a> for more information on Pages Functions pricing.</p></div></div></aside>
<div tabindex="-1" class="heading-wrapper level-h2"><h2 id="workers">Workers</h2><a class="anchor-link" href="#workers"><span aria-hidden="true" class="anchor-icon"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path fill="currentcolor" d="m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z"></path></svg></span></a></div>
<p>Users on the Workers Paid plan have access to the Standard usage model. Workers Enterprise accounts are billed based on the usage model specified in their contract. To switch to the Standard usage model, contact your Account Manager.</p>
<div class="table-scroll" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="Table"><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th></th>
<th>Requests<sup>1, 2, 3, 4</sup></th>
<th>Duration</th>
<th>CPU time</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><strong>Free</strong></td>
<td>100,000 per day</td>
<td>No charge for duration</td>
<td>10 milliseconds of CPU time per invocation</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>Standard</strong></td>
<td>10 million included per month <br> +$0.30 per additional million</td>
<td>No charge or limit for duration</td>
<td>30 million CPU milliseconds included per month<br> +$0.02 per additional million CPU milliseconds<br><br> Max of <a href="/workers/platform/limits/#account-plan-limits">5 minutes of CPU time</a> per invocation (default: 30 seconds)<br> Max of 15 minutes of CPU time per <a href="/workers/configuration/cron-triggers/">Cron Trigger</a> or <a href="/queues/configuration/javascript-apis/#consumer">Queue Consumer</a> invocation</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></div>
<p><sup>1</sup> Inbound requests to your Worker. Cloudflare does not bill for
<a href="/workers/platform/limits/#subrequests">subrequests</a> you make from your Worker.</p>
<p><sup>2</sup> WebSocket connections made to a Worker are charged as a request,
representing the initial <code>Upgrade</code> connection made to establish the WebSocket.
WebSocket messages routed through a Worker do not count as requests.</p>
<p><sup>3</sup> Requests to static assets are free and unlimited.</p>
<p><sup>4</sup> When <a href="/workers/cache/">Workers Caching</a> is enabled, requests served
from the Worker's cache are billed at the same per-request rate as requests that
invoke the Worker. This includes requests to <a href="/workers/static-assets/billing-and-limitations/">static
assets</a> and <a href="/workers/platform/pricing/#service-bindings">worker-to-worker
invocations</a>.
CPU time is only billed when the Worker runs (on a
cache miss or bypass).</p>
<div tabindex="-1" class="heading-wrapper level-h3"><h3 id="example-pricing">Example pricing</h3><a class="anchor-link" href="#example-pricing"><span aria-hidden="true" class="anchor-icon"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path fill="currentcolor" d="m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z"></path></svg></span></a></div>
<div tabindex="-1" class="heading-wrapper level-h4"><h4 id="example-1">Example 1</h4><a class="anchor-link" href="#example-1"><span aria-hidden="true" class="anchor-icon"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path fill="currentcolor" d="m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z"></path></svg></span></a></div>
<p>A Worker that serves 15 million requests per month, and uses an average of 7 milliseconds (ms) of CPU time per request, would have the following estimated costs:</p>

<div tabindex="-1" class="heading-wrapper level-h4"><h4 id="example-2">Example 2</h4><a class="anchor-link" href="#example-2"><span aria-hidden="true" class="anchor-icon"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path fill="currentcolor" d="m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z"></path></svg></span></a></div>
<p>A project that serves 15 million requests per month, with 80% (12 million) requests serving <a href="/workers/static-assets/">static assets</a> and the remaining invoking dynamic Worker code. The Worker uses an average of 7 milliseconds (ms) of time per request.</p>
<p>Requests to static assets are free and unlimited. This project would have the following estimated costs:</p>

<aside role="note" aria-label="Custom limits" class="aside-card flex items-start gap-3 rounded-lg px-4 py-3 my-4" style="--_c: var(--nb-info); --_t: var(--nb-info-muted);" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><span class="flex h-[1.375em] shrink-0 items-center" aria-hidden="true" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><svg width="1em" height="1em" viewBox="0 0 256 256" class="h-[1em] w-[1em]" data-astro-cid-znle5jil="true" data-icon="ph:info"><path fill="currentColor" d="M128 24a104 104 0 1 0 104 104A104.11 104.11 0 0 0 128 24m0 192a88 88 0 1 1 88-88a88.1 88.1 0 0 1-88 88m16-40a8 8 0 0 1-8 8a16 16 0 0 1-16-16v-40a8 8 0 0 1 0-16a16 16 0 0 1 16 16v40a8 8 0 0 1 8 8m-32-92a12 12 0 1 1 12 12a12 12 0 0 1-12-12"/></svg></span><div class="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col gap-0.5" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><p class="m-0 text-base leading-snug font-semibold" data-astro-cid-znle5jil>Custom limits</p><div class="aside-card-body text-sm leading-normal" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><p>To prevent accidental runaway bills or denial-of-wallet attacks, configure the maximum amount of CPU time that can be used per invocation by <a href="/workers/wrangler/configuration/#limits">defining limits in your Worker's Wrangler file</a>, or via the Cloudflare dashboard (<strong>Workers &amp; Pages</strong> &gt; Select your Worker &gt; <strong>Settings</strong> &gt; <strong>CPU Limits</strong>).</p><p>If you had a Worker on the Bundled usage model prior to the migration to Standard pricing on March 1, 2024, Cloudflare has automatically added a 50 ms CPU limit on your Worker.</p></div></div></aside>
<div tabindex="-1" class="heading-wrapper level-h3"><h3 id="how-to-switch-usage-models">How to switch usage models</h3><a class="anchor-link" href="#how-to-switch-usage-models"><span aria-hidden="true" class="anchor-icon"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path fill="currentcolor" d="m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z"></path></svg></span></a></div>
<aside role="note" aria-label="Note" class="aside-card flex items-start gap-3 rounded-lg px-4 py-3 my-4" style="--_c: var(--nb-info); --_t: var(--nb-info-muted);" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><span class="flex h-[1.375em] shrink-0 items-center" aria-hidden="true" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><svg width="1em" height="1em" viewBox="0 0 256 256" class="h-[1em] w-[1em]" data-astro-cid-znle5jil="true" data-icon="ph:info"><path fill="currentColor" d="M128 24a104 104 0 1 0 104 104A104.11 104.11 0 0 0 128 24m0 192a88 88 0 1 1 88-88a88.1 88.1 0 0 1-88 88m16-40a8 8 0 0 1-8 8a16 16 0 0 1-16-16v-40a8 8 0 0 1 0-16a16 16 0 0 1 16 16v40a8 8 0 0 1 8 8m-32-92a12 12 0 1 1 12 12a12 12 0 0 1-12-12"/></svg></span><div class="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col gap-0.5" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><p class="m-0 text-base leading-snug font-semibold" data-astro-cid-znle5jil>Note</p><div class="aside-card-body text-sm leading-normal" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><p>Some Workers Enterprise customers maintain the ability to change usage models.</p></div></div></aside>
<p>Users on the Workers Paid plan have access to the Standard usage model. However, some users may still have a legacy usage model configured.
Legacy usage models include Workers Unbound and Workers Bundled. Users are advised to move to the Workers Standard usage model.
Changing the usage model only affects billable usage, and has no technical implications.</p>
<p>To change your default account-wide usage model:</p>
<ol>
<li>
<p>In the Cloudflare dashboard, go to the <strong>Workers &amp; Pages</strong> page.</p>
<a href="https://dash.cloudflare.com/?to=/:account/workers-and-pages" data-nb-button class="group inline-flex w-max shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full font-medium whitespace-nowrap no-underline shadow-xs transition-colors cursor-pointer select-none focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-ring focus-visible:outline-offset-2 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50 bg-primary text-primary-foreground hover:bg-primary-hover h-9 gap-1.5 px-3 text-sm" target="_blank">
	Go to <strong>Workers &amp; Pages</strong>&nbsp;&#8599;
</a>
</li>
<li>
<p>Find <strong>Usage Model</strong> on the right-side menu &gt; <strong>Change</strong>.</p>
</li>
</ol>
<p>Usage models may be changed at the individual Worker level:</p>
<ol>
<li>
<p>In the Cloudflare dashboard, go to the <strong>Workers &amp; Pages</strong> page.</p>
<a href="https://dash.cloudflare.com/?to=/:account/workers-and-pages" data-nb-button class="group inline-flex w-max shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full font-medium whitespace-nowrap no-underline shadow-xs transition-colors cursor-pointer select-none focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-ring focus-visible:outline-offset-2 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50 bg-primary text-primary-foreground hover:bg-primary-hover h-9 gap-1.5 px-3 text-sm" target="_blank">
	Go to <strong>Workers &amp; Pages</strong>&nbsp;&#8599;
</a>
</li>
<li>
<p>In <strong>Overview</strong>, select your Worker &gt; <strong>Settings</strong> &gt; <strong>Usage Model</strong>.</p>
</li>
</ol>
<p>Existing Workers will not be impacted when changing the default usage model. You may change the usage model for individual Workers without affecting your account-wide default usage model.</p>

usage limits reset daily at 00:00 UTC. If you exceed any one of these limits,
further operations of that type will fail with an error.</p>
<aside role="note" aria-label="Note" class="aside-card flex items-start gap-3 rounded-lg px-4 py-3 my-4" style="--_c: var(--nb-info); --_t: var(--nb-info-muted);" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><span class="flex h-[1.375em] shrink-0 items-center" aria-hidden="true" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><svg width="1em" height="1em" viewBox="0 0 256 256" class="h-[1em] w-[1em]" data-astro-cid-znle5jil="true" data-icon="ph:info"><path fill="currentColor" d="M128 24a104 104 0 1 0 104 104A104.11 104.11 0 0 0 128 24m0 192a88 88 0 1 1 88-88a88.1 88.1 0 0 1-88 88m16-40a8 8 0 0 1-8 8a16 16 0 0 1-16-16v-40a8 8 0 0 1 0-16a16 16 0 0 1 16 16v40a8 8 0 0 1 8 8m-32-92a12 12 0 1 1 12 12a12 12 0 0 1-12-12"/></svg></span><div class="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col gap-0.5" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><p class="m-0 text-base leading-snug font-semibold" data-astro-cid-znle5jil>Note</p><div class="aside-card-body text-sm leading-normal" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><p>Workers KV pricing for read, write and delete operations is on a per-key basis. Bulk read operations
are billed by the amount of keys read in a bulk read operation.</p></div></div></aside>
<aside role="note" aria-label="KV documentation" class="aside-card flex items-start gap-3 rounded-lg px-4 py-3 my-4" style="--_c: var(--nb-info); --_t: var(--nb-info-muted);" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><span class="flex h-[1.375em] shrink-0 items-center" aria-hidden="true" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><svg width="1em" height="1em" viewBox="0 0 256 256" class="h-[1em] w-[1em]" data-astro-cid-znle5jil="true" data-icon="ph:info"><path fill="currentColor" d="M128 24a104 104 0 1 0 104 104A104.11 104.11 0 0 0 128 24m0 192a88 88 0 1 1 88-88a88.1 88.1 0 0 1-88 88m16-40a8 8 0 0 1-8 8a16 16 0 0 1-16-16v-40a8 8 0 0 1 0-16a16 16 0 0 1 16 16v40a8 8 0 0 1 8 8m-32-92a12 12 0 1 1 12 12a12 12 0 0 1-12-12"/></svg></span><div class="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col gap-0.5" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><p class="m-0 text-base leading-snug font-semibold" data-astro-cid-znle5jil>KV documentation</p><div class="aside-card-body text-sm leading-normal" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><p>To learn more about KV, refer to the <a href="/kv/">KV documentation</a>.</p></div></div></aside>
<div tabindex="-1" class="heading-wrapper level-h2"><h2 id="hyperdrive">Hyperdrive</h2><a class="anchor-link" href="#hyperdrive"><span aria-hidden="true" class="anchor-icon"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path fill="currentcolor" d="m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z"></path></svg></span></a></div>

<aside role="note" aria-label="Hyperdrive documentation" class="aside-card flex items-start gap-3 rounded-lg px-4 py-3 my-4" style="--_c: var(--nb-info); --_t: var(--nb-info-muted);" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><span class="flex h-[1.375em] shrink-0 items-center" aria-hidden="true" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><svg width="1em" height="1em" viewBox="0 0 256 256" class="h-[1em] w-[1em]" data-astro-cid-znle5jil="true" data-icon="ph:info"><path fill="currentColor" d="M128 24a104 104 0 1 0 104 104A104.11 104.11 0 0 0 128 24m0 192a88 88 0 1 1 88-88a88.1 88.1 0 0 1-88 88m16-40a8 8 0 0 1-8 8a16 16 0 0 1-16-16v-40a8 8 0 0 1 0-16a16 16 0 0 1 16 16v40a8 8 0 0 1 8 8m-32-92a12 12 0 1 1 12 12a12 12 0 0 1-12-12"/></svg></span><div class="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col gap-0.5" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><p class="m-0 text-base leading-snug font-semibold" data-astro-cid-znle5jil>Hyperdrive documentation</p><div class="aside-card-body text-sm leading-normal" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><p>To learn more about Hyperdrive, refer to the <a href="/hyperdrive/">Hyperdrive documentation</a>.</p></div></div></aside>
<div tabindex="-1" class="heading-wrapper level-h2"><h2 id="queues">Queues</h2><a class="anchor-link" href="#queues"><span aria-hidden="true" class="anchor-icon"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path fill="currentcolor" d="m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z"></path></svg></span></a></div>
<p>Cloudflare Queues charges for operations and for queues above the included amount on the Workers Paid plan.</p>
<ul>
<li>An operation is counted for each 64 KB of data that is written, read, or deleted.</li>
<li>Messages larger than 64 KB are charged as if they were multiple messages: for example, a 65 KB message and a 127 KB message would both incur two operation charges when written, read, or deleted.</li>
<li>A KB is defined as 1,000 bytes, and each message includes approximately 100 bytes of internal metadata.</li>
<li>Operations are per message, not per batch. A batch of 10 messages (the default batch size), if processed, would incur 10x write, 10x read, and 10x delete operations: one for each message in the batch.</li>
<li>There are no data transfer (egress) or throughput (bandwidth) charges.</li>
</ul>
<div class="table-scroll" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="Table"><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th></th>
<th>Workers Free</th>
<th>Workers Paid</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td>Standard operations</td>
<td>10,000 operations/day included</td>
<td>1,000,000 operations/month included + $0.40/million operations</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Queues per account</td>
<td>10,000</td>
<td>Unlimited, with a default limit of 10,000. First 10,000 included + $0.10 per additional queue per month.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Message retention</td>
<td>24 hours (non-configurable)</td>
<td>4 days default, configurable up to 14 days</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></div>
<p>Refer to <a href="/queues/platform/limits/">Queues limits</a> for queue creation limits and how to request a limit increase.</p>
<p>In most cases, it takes 3 operations to deliver a message: 1 write, 1 read, and 1 delete. Therefore, you can use the following formula to estimate your monthly operations charges, excluding charges for additional queues:</p>
<figure class="nb-code-figure" data-nb-lang="txt"><pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark nb-shiki-c6xiwz" tabindex="0" data-language="txt" data-nb-lang="txt"><code><span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-wvjl67">((Number of Messages * 3) - 1,000,000) / 1,000,000  * $0.40</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>Additionally:</p>
<ul>
<li>Each retry incurs a read operation. A batch of 10 messages that is retried would incur 10 operations for each retry.</li>
<li>Messages that reach the maximum retries and that are written to a <a href="/queues/configuration/batching-retries/">Dead Letter Queue</a> incur a write operation for each 64 KB chunk. A message that was retried 3 times (the default), fails delivery on the fourth time and is written to a Dead Letter Queue would incur five (5) read operations.</li>
<li>Messages that are written to a queue, but that reach the maximum persistence duration (or "expire") before they are read, incur only a write and delete operation per 64 KB chunk.</li>
</ul>
<aside role="note" aria-label="Queues billing examples" class="aside-card flex items-start gap-3 rounded-lg px-4 py-3 my-4" style="--_c: var(--nb-info); --_t: var(--nb-info-muted);" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><span class="flex h-[1.375em] shrink-0 items-center" aria-hidden="true" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><svg width="1em" height="1em" viewBox="0 0 256 256" class="h-[1em] w-[1em]" data-astro-cid-znle5jil="true" data-icon="ph:info"><path fill="currentColor" d="M128 24a104 104 0 1 0 104 104A104.11 104.11 0 0 0 128 24m0 192a88 88 0 1 1 88-88a88.1 88.1 0 0 1-88 88m16-40a8 8 0 0 1-8 8a16 16 0 0 1-16-16v-40a8 8 0 0 1 0-16a16 16 0 0 1 16 16v40a8 8 0 0 1 8 8m-32-92a12 12 0 1 1 12 12a12 12 0 0 1-12-12"/></svg></span><div class="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col gap-0.5" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><p class="m-0 text-base leading-snug font-semibold" data-astro-cid-znle5jil>Queues billing examples</p><div class="aside-card-body text-sm leading-normal" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><p>To learn more about Queues pricing and review billing examples, refer to <a href="/queues/platform/pricing/">Queues Pricing</a>.</p></div></div></aside>
<div tabindex="-1" class="heading-wrapper level-h2"><h2 id="workflows">Workflows</h2><a class="anchor-link" href="#workflows"><span aria-hidden="true" class="anchor-icon"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path fill="currentcolor" d="m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z"></path></svg></span></a></div>

<aside role="note" aria-label="Workflows pricing" class="aside-card flex items-start gap-3 rounded-lg px-4 py-3 my-4" style="--_c: var(--nb-info); --_t: var(--nb-info-muted);" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><span class="flex h-[1.375em] shrink-0 items-center" aria-hidden="true" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><svg width="1em" height="1em" viewBox="0 0 256 256" class="h-[1em] w-[1em]" data-astro-cid-znle5jil="true" data-icon="ph:info"><path fill="currentColor" d="M128 24a104 104 0 1 0 104 104A104.11 104.11 0 0 0 128 24m0 192a88 88 0 1 1 88-88a88.1 88.1 0 0 1-88 88m16-40a8 8 0 0 1-8 8a16 16 0 0 1-16-16v-40a8 8 0 0 1 0-16a16 16 0 0 1 16 16v40a8 8 0 0 1 8 8m-32-92a12 12 0 1 1 12 12a12 12 0 0 1-12-12"/></svg></span><div class="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col gap-0.5" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><p class="m-0 text-base leading-snug font-semibold" data-astro-cid-znle5jil>Workflows pricing</p><div class="aside-card-body text-sm leading-normal" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><p>To learn more about Workflows billing, refer to <a href="/workflows/reference/pricing/">Workflows pricing</a>.</p></div></div></aside>
<div tabindex="-1" class="heading-wrapper level-h2"><h2 id="d1">D1</h2><a class="anchor-link" href="#d1"><span aria-hidden="true" class="anchor-icon"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path fill="currentcolor" d="m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z"></path></svg></span></a></div>

<aside role="note" aria-label="Track your D1 usage" class="aside-card flex items-start gap-3 rounded-lg px-4 py-3 my-4" style="--_c: var(--nb-info); --_t: var(--nb-info-muted);" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><span class="flex h-[1.375em] shrink-0 items-center" aria-hidden="true" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><svg width="1em" height="1em" viewBox="0 0 256 256" class="h-[1em] w-[1em]" data-astro-cid-znle5jil="true" data-icon="ph:info"><path fill="currentColor" d="M128 24a104 104 0 1 0 104 104A104.11 104.11 0 0 0 128 24m0 192a88 88 0 1 1 88-88a88.1 88.1 0 0 1-88 88m16-40a8 8 0 0 1-8 8a16 16 0 0 1-16-16v-40a8 8 0 0 1 0-16a16 16 0 0 1 16 16v40a8 8 0 0 1 8 8m-32-92a12 12 0 1 1 12 12a12 12 0 0 1-12-12"/></svg></span><div class="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col gap-0.5" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><p class="m-0 text-base leading-snug font-semibold" data-astro-cid-znle5jil>Track your D1 usage</p><div class="aside-card-body text-sm leading-normal" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><p>To accurately track your usage, use the <a href="/d1/worker-api/return-object/">meta object</a>, <a href="/d1/observability/metrics-analytics/#query-via-the-graphql-api">GraphQL Analytics API</a>, or the <a href="https://dash.cloudflare.com/?to=/:account/workers/d1/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Cloudflare dashboard<span class="external-link"> âï¸</span></a>. Select your D1 database, then view: Metrics &gt; Row Metrics.</p></div></div></aside>
<div tabindex="-1" class="heading-wrapper level-h3"><h3 id="definitions">Definitions</h3><a class="anchor-link" href="#definitions"><span aria-hidden="true" class="anchor-icon"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path fill="currentcolor" d="m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z"></path></svg></span></a></div>
<ol>
<li>Rows read measure how many rows a query reads (scans), regardless of the size of each row. For example, if you have a table with 5000 rows and run a <code>SELECT * FROM table</code> as a full table scan, this would count as 5,000 rows read. A query that filters on an <a href="/d1/best-practices/use-indexes/">unindexed column</a> may return fewer rows to your Worker, but is still required to read (scan) more rows to determine which subset to return.</li>
<li>Rows written measure how many rows were written to D1 database. Write operations include <code>INSERT</code>, <code>UPDATE</code>, and <code>DELETE</code>. Each of these operations contribute towards rows written. A query that <code>INSERT</code> 10 rows into a <code>users</code> table would count as 10 rows written.</li>
<li>DDL operations (for example, <code>CREATE</code>, <code>ALTER</code>, and <code>DROP</code>) are used to define or modify the structure of a database. They may contribute to a mix of read rows and write rows. Ensure you are accurately tracking your usage through the available tools (<a href="/d1/worker-api/return-object/">meta object</a>, <a href="/d1/observability/metrics-analytics/#query-via-the-graphql-api">GraphQL Analytics API</a>, or the <a href="https://dash.cloudflare.com/?to=/:account/workers/d1/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Cloudflare dashboard<span class="external-link"> âï¸</span></a>).</li>
<li>Row size or the number of columns in a row does not impact how rows are counted. A row that is 1 KB and a row that is 100 KB both count as one row.</li>
<li>Defining <a href="/d1/best-practices/use-indexes/">indexes</a> on your table(s) reduces the number of rows read by a query when filtering on that indexed field. For example, if the <code>users</code> table has an index on a timestamp column <code>created_at</code>, the query <code>SELECT * FROM users WHERE created_at &gt; ?1</code> would only need to read a subset of the table.</li>
<li>Indexes will add an additional written row when writes include the indexed column, as there are two rows written: one to the table itself, and one to the index. The performance benefit of an index and reduction in rows read will, in nearly all cases, offset this additional write.</li>
<li>Storage is based on gigabytes stored per month, and is based on the sum of all databases in your account. Tables and indexes both count towards storage consumed.</li>
<li>Free daily usage limits reset at 00:00 UTC. Monthly included usage resets based on your monthly subscription renewal date, which is determined by the day you first subscribed.</li>
<li>There are no data transfer (egress) or throughput (bandwidth) charges for data accessed from D1.</li>
<li><a href="/d1/best-practices/read-replication/">Read replication</a> does not charge extra for read replicas. You incur the same usage billing based on <code>rows_read</code> and <code>rows_written</code> by your queries.</li>
</ol>
<aside role="note" aria-label="D1 billing" class="aside-card flex items-start gap-3 rounded-lg px-4 py-3 my-4" style="--_c: var(--nb-info); --_t: var(--nb-info-muted);" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><span class="flex h-[1.375em] shrink-0 items-center" aria-hidden="true" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><svg width="1em" height="1em" viewBox="0 0 256 256" class="h-[1em] w-[1em]" data-astro-cid-znle5jil="true" data-icon="ph:info"><path fill="currentColor" d="M128 24a104 104 0 1 0 104 104A104.11 104.11 0 0 0 128 24m0 192a88 88 0 1 1 88-88a88.1 88.1 0 0 1-88 88m16-40a8 8 0 0 1-8 8a16 16 0 0 1-16-16v-40a8 8 0 0 1 0-16a16 16 0 0 1 16 16v40a8 8 0 0 1 8 8m-32-92a12 12 0 1 1 12 12a12 12 0 0 1-12-12"/></svg></span><div class="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col gap-0.5" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><p class="m-0 text-base leading-snug font-semibold" data-astro-cid-znle5jil>D1 billing</p><div class="aside-card-body text-sm leading-normal" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><p>Refer to <a href="/d1/platform/pricing/">D1 Pricing</a> to learn more about how D1 is billed.</p></div></div></aside>
<div tabindex="-1" class="heading-wrapper level-h2"><h2 id="durable-objects">Durable Objects</h2><a class="anchor-link" href="#durable-objects"><span aria-hidden="true" class="anchor-icon"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path fill="currentcolor" d="m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z"></path></svg></span></a></div>
<aside role="note" aria-label="Note" class="aside-card flex items-start gap-3 rounded-lg px-4 py-3 my-4" style="--_c: var(--nb-info); --_t: var(--nb-info-muted);" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><span class="flex h-[1.375em] shrink-0 items-center" aria-hidden="true" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><svg width="1em" height="1em" viewBox="0 0 256 256" class="h-[1em] w-[1em]" data-astro-cid-znle5jil="true" data-icon="ph:info"><path fill="currentColor" d="M128 24a104 104 0 1 0 104 104A104.11 104.11 0 0 0 128 24m0 192a88 88 0 1 1 88-88a88.1 88.1 0 0 1-88 88m16-40a8 8 0 0 1-8 8a16 16 0 0 1-16-16v-40a8 8 0 0 1 0-16a16 16 0 0 1 16 16v40a8 8 0 0 1 8 8m-32-92a12 12 0 1 1 12 12a12 12 0 0 1-12-12"/></svg></span><div class="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col gap-0.5" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><p class="m-0 text-base leading-snug font-semibold" data-astro-cid-znle5jil>Note</p><div class="aside-card-body text-sm leading-normal" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><p>Durable Objects are available both on Workers Free and Workers Paid plans.</p><ul>
<li><strong>Workers Free plan</strong>: Only Durable Objects with <a href="/durable-objects/best-practices/access-durable-objects-storage/#create-sqlite-backed-durable-object-class">SQLite storage backend</a> are available.</li>
<li><strong>Workers Paid plan</strong>: Durable Objects with the SQLite storage backend are available. The <a href="/durable-objects/reference/durable-objects-migrations/#storage-backends">key-value storage backend</a> is only available to accounts that already have a key-value-backed namespace.</li>
</ul><p>If you wish to downgrade from a Workers Paid plan to a Workers Free plan, you must first ensure that you have deleted all Durable Object namespaces with the key-value storage backend.</p></div></div></aside>
<div tabindex="-1" class="heading-wrapper level-h3"><h3 id="compute-billing">Compute billing</h3><a class="anchor-link" href="#compute-billing"><span aria-hidden="true" class="anchor-icon"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path fill="currentcolor" d="m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z"></path></svg></span></a></div>

<p>Durable Objects are billed for compute duration (wall-clock time) while the Durable Object is actively running or is idle in memory but unable to <a href="/durable-objects/concepts/durable-object-lifecycle/">hibernate</a>. Durable Objects that are idle and eligible for hibernation are not billed for duration, even before the runtime has hibernated them. Requests to a Durable Object keep it active or create the object if it was inactive.</p>
<p>For each metered dimension, billable usage is the amount consumed in excess of the included monthly allocation. This billable usage is rounded up to the next billable unit before the corresponding rate is applied. For example, 500,000 GB-s of billable compute duration is rounded up to 1,000,000 GB-s and billed accordingly.</p>
<div class="table-scroll" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="Table"><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th></th>
<th>Free plan</th>
<th>Paid plan</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td>Requests</td>
<td>100,000 / day</td>
<td>1 million / month, + $0.15/million<br> Includes HTTP requests, RPC sessions<sup>1</sup>, WebSocket messages<sup>2</sup>, and alarm invocations</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Duration<sup>3</sup></td>
<td>13,000 GB-s / day</td>
<td>400,000 GB-s / month, + $12.50/million GB-s<sup>4,5</sup></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></div>
<details open class="border-border my-4 rounded-lg border px-4 py-2" data-astro-cid-nziubcoq><summary class="cursor-pointer font-medium" data-astro-cid-nziubcoq><p>Footnotes</p>
</summary><p><sup>1</sup> Each <a href="/workers/runtime-apis/rpc/lifecycle/">RPC session</a> is billed as one request to your Durable Object. Every <a href="/durable-objects/best-practices/create-durable-object-stubs-and-send-requests/">RPC method call</a> on a <a href="/durable-objects/">Durable Objects stub</a> is its own RPC session and therefore a single billed request.</p><p>RPC method calls can return objects (stubs) extending <a href="/workers/runtime-apis/rpc/lifecycle/#lifetimes-memory-and-resource-management"><code>RpcTarget</code></a> and invoke calls on those stubs. Subsequent calls on the returned stub are part of the same RPC session and are not billed as separate requests. For example:</p><figure class="nb-code-figure" data-nb-lang="js"><pre class="astro-code astro-code-themes github-light github-dark nb-shiki-c6xiwz" tabindex="0" data-language="js" data-nb-lang="js"><code><span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-1itgoe">let</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh"> durableObjectStub </span><span class="nb-shiki-1itgoe">=</span><span class="nb-shiki-dzsirb"> OBJECT_NAMESPACE</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">.</span><span class="nb-shiki-1t8gfj">get</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">(id); </span><span class="nb-shiki-21nrsd">// retrieve Durable Object stub</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-1itgoe">using</span><span class="nb-shiki-dzsirb"> foo</span><span class="nb-shiki-1itgoe"> =</span><span class="nb-shiki-1itgoe"> await</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh"> durableObjectStub.</span><span class="nb-shiki-1t8gfj">bar</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">(); </span><span class="nb-shiki-21nrsd">// billed as a request</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-1itgoe">await</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh"> foo.</span><span class="nb-shiki-1t8gfj">baz</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">(); </span><span class="nb-shiki-21nrsd">// treated as part of the same RPC session created by calling bar(), not billed as a request</span></span>
<span class="line"><span class="nb-shiki-1itgoe">await</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh"> durableObjectStub.</span><span class="nb-shiki-1t8gfj">cat</span><span class="nb-shiki-140thh">(); </span><span class="nb-shiki-21nrsd">// billed as a request</span></span></code></pre></figure><p><sup>2</sup> A request is needed to create a WebSocket connection. There is no charge for outgoing WebSocket messages, nor for incoming <a href="https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc6455#section-5.5.2" target="_blank" rel="noopener">WebSocket protocol pings<span class="external-link"> âï¸</span></a>. For compute requests billing-only, a 20:1 ratio is applied to incoming WebSocket messages to factor in smaller messages for real-time communication. For example, 100 WebSocket incoming messages would be charged as 5 requests for billing purposes. The 20:1 ratio does not affect Durable Object metrics and analytics, which reflect actual usage.</p><p><sup>3</sup> Application level auto-response messages handled by <a href="/durable-objects/best-practices/websockets/"><code>state.setWebSocketAutoResponse()</code></a> will not incur additional wall-clock time, and so they will not be charged.</p><p><sup>4</sup> Duration is billed in wall-clock time as long as the Object is active and not eligible for hibernation, but is shared across all requests active on an Object at once. Calling <code>accept()</code> on a WebSocket in an Object will incur duration charges for the entire time the WebSocket is connected. It is recommended to use the WebSocket Hibernation API to avoid incurring duration charges once all event handlers finish running. For a complete explanation, refer to <a href="/durable-objects/platform/pricing/#when-does-a-durable-object-incur-duration-charges">When does a Durable Object incur duration charges?</a>.</p><p><sup>5</sup> Duration billing charges for the 128 MB of memory your Durable Object is allocated, regardless of actual usage. If your account creates many instances of a single Durable Object class, Durable Objects may run in the same isolate on the same physical machine and share the 128 MB of memory. These Durable Objects are still billed as if they are allocated a full 128 MB of memory.</p></details>
<div tabindex="-1" class="heading-wrapper level-h3"><h3 id="storage-billing">Storage billing</h3><a class="anchor-link" href="#storage-billing"><span aria-hidden="true" class="anchor-icon"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path fill="currentcolor" d="m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z"></path></svg></span></a></div>

<p>The <a href="/durable-objects/api/sqlite-storage-api/">Durable Objects Storage API</a> is only accessible from within Durable Objects. Pricing depends on the storage backend of your Durable Objects.</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>SQLite-backed Durable Objects (recommended)</strong>: <a href="/durable-objects/best-practices/access-durable-objects-storage/#create-sqlite-backed-durable-object-class">SQLite storage backend</a> is recommended for all new Durable Object classes. Workers Free plan can only create and access SQLite-backed Durable Objects.</li>
<li><strong>Key-value backed Durable Objects</strong>: <a href="/durable-objects/reference/durable-object-class-migrations-legacy/#create-durable-object-class-with-key-value-storage">Key-value storage backend</a> is only available on the Workers Paid plan.</li>
</ul>
<div tabindex="-1" class="heading-wrapper level-h4"><h4 id="sqlite-storage-backend">SQLite storage backend</h4><a class="anchor-link" href="#sqlite-storage-backend"><span aria-hidden="true" class="anchor-icon"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path fill="currentcolor" d="m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z"></path></svg></span></a></div>

<aside role="note" aria-label="Storage billing on SQLite-backed Durable Objects" class="aside-card flex items-start gap-3 rounded-lg px-4 py-3 my-4" style="--_c: var(--nb-info); --_t: var(--nb-info-muted);" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><span class="flex h-[1.375em] shrink-0 items-center" aria-hidden="true" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><svg width="1em" height="1em" viewBox="0 0 256 256" class="h-[1em] w-[1em]" data-astro-cid-znle5jil="true" data-icon="ph:info"><path fill="currentColor" d="M128 24a104 104 0 1 0 104 104A104.11 104.11 0 0 0 128 24m0 192a88 88 0 1 1 88-88a88.1 88.1 0 0 1-88 88m16-40a8 8 0 0 1-8 8a16 16 0 0 1-16-16v-40a8 8 0 0 1 0-16a16 16 0 0 1 16 16v40a8 8 0 0 1 8 8m-32-92a12 12 0 1 1 12 12a12 12 0 0 1-12-12"/></svg></span><div class="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col gap-0.5" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><p class="m-0 text-base leading-snug font-semibold" data-astro-cid-znle5jil>Storage billing on SQLite-backed Durable Objects</p><div class="aside-card-body text-sm leading-normal" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><p>Storage billing for SQLite-backed Durable Objects will be enabled in January 2026, with a target date of January 7, 2026 (no earlier). Only SQLite storage usage on and after the billing target date will incur charges. For more information, refer to <a href="/changelog/post/2025-12-12-durable-objects-sqlite-storage-billing/">Billing for SQLite Storage</a>.</p></div></div></aside>

<details open class="border-border my-4 rounded-lg border px-4 py-2" data-astro-cid-nziubcoq><summary class="cursor-pointer font-medium" data-astro-cid-nziubcoq><p>Footnotes</p>
</summary><p><sup>1</sup> Rows read and rows written included limits and rates match <a href="/d1/platform/pricing/">D1 pricing</a>, Cloudflare's serverless SQL database.</p><p><sup>2</sup> Key-value methods like <code>get()</code>, <code>put()</code>, <code>delete()</code>, or <code>list()</code> store and query data in a hidden SQLite table and are billed as rows read and rows written.</p><p><sup>3</sup> Each <code>setAlarm()</code> is billed as a single row written.</p><p><sup>4</sup> Deletes are counted as rows written.</p><p><sup>5</sup> Durable Objects will be billed for stored data until the <a href="/durable-objects/best-practices/access-durable-objects-storage/#remove-a-durable-objects-storage">data is removed</a>. Once the data is removed, the object will be cleaned up automatically by the system.</p></details>
<div tabindex="-1" class="heading-wrapper level-h4"><h4 id="key-value-storage-backend">Key-value storage backend</h4><a class="anchor-link" href="#key-value-storage-backend"><span aria-hidden="true" class="anchor-icon"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path fill="currentcolor" d="m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z"></path></svg></span></a></div>

<details open class="border-border my-4 rounded-lg border px-4 py-2" data-astro-cid-nziubcoq><summary class="cursor-pointer font-medium" data-astro-cid-nziubcoq><p>Footnotes</p>
</summary><p><sup>1</sup> A request unit is defined as 4 KB of data read or written. A request that writes or reads more than 4 KB will consume multiple units, for example, a 9 KB write will consume 3 write request units.</p><p><sup>2</sup> List operations are billed by read request units, based on the amount of data examined. For example, a list request that returns a combined 80 KB of keys and values will be billed 20 read request units. A list request that does not return anything is billed for 1 read request unit.</p><p><sup>3</sup> Each <code>setAlarm</code> is billed as a single write request unit.</p><p><sup>4</sup> Delete requests are unmetered. For example, deleting a 100 KB value will be charged one delete request.</p><p><sup>5</sup> Durable Objects will be billed for stored data until the data is removed. Once the data is removed, the object will be cleaned up automatically by the system.</p><p>Requests that hit the <a href="/durable-objects/reference/in-memory-state/">Durable Objects in-memory cache</a> or that use the <a href="/durable-objects/api/sqlite-storage-api/">multi-key versions of <code>get()</code>/<code>put()</code>/<code>delete()</code> methods</a> are billed the same as if they were a normal, individual request for each key.</p></details>
<aside role="note" aria-label="Durable Objects billing examples" class="aside-card flex items-start gap-3 rounded-lg px-4 py-3 my-4" style="--_c: var(--nb-info); --_t: var(--nb-info-muted);" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><span class="flex h-[1.375em] shrink-0 items-center" aria-hidden="true" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><svg width="1em" height="1em" viewBox="0 0 256 256" class="h-[1em] w-[1em]" data-astro-cid-znle5jil="true" data-icon="ph:info"><path fill="currentColor" d="M128 24a104 104 0 1 0 104 104A104.11 104.11 0 0 0 128 24m0 192a88 88 0 1 1 88-88a88.1 88.1 0 0 1-88 88m16-40a8 8 0 0 1-8 8a16 16 0 0 1-16-16v-40a8 8 0 0 1 0-16a16 16 0 0 1 16 16v40a8 8 0 0 1 8 8m-32-92a12 12 0 1 1 12 12a12 12 0 0 1-12-12"/></svg></span><div class="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col gap-0.5" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><p class="m-0 text-base leading-snug font-semibold" data-astro-cid-znle5jil>Durable Objects billing examples</p><div class="aside-card-body text-sm leading-normal" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><p>For more information and <a href="/durable-objects/platform/pricing#compute-billing-examples">examples of Durable Objects billing</a>, refer to <a href="/durable-objects/platform/pricing/">Durable Objects Pricing</a>.</p></div></div></aside>
<div tabindex="-1" class="heading-wrapper level-h2"><h2 id="vectorize">Vectorize</h2><a class="anchor-link" href="#vectorize"><span aria-hidden="true" class="anchor-icon"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path fill="currentcolor" d="m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z"></path></svg></span></a></div>
<p>Vectorize is currently only available on the Workers paid plan.</p>

<div tabindex="-1" class="heading-wrapper level-h3"><h3 id="calculating-vector-dimensions">Calculating vector dimensions</h3><a class="anchor-link" href="#calculating-vector-dimensions"><span aria-hidden="true" class="anchor-icon"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path fill="currentcolor" d="m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z"></path></svg></span></a></div>
<p>To calculate your potential usage, calculate the queried vector dimensions and the stored vector dimensions, and multiply by the unit price. The formula is defined as <code>((queried vectors + stored vectors) * dimensions * ($0.01 / 1,000,000)) + (stored vectors * dimensions * ($0.05 / 100,000,000))</code></p>
<ul>
<li>For example, inserting 10,000 vectors of 768 dimensions each, and querying those 1,000 times per day (30,000 times per month) would be calculated as <code>((30,000 + 10,000) * 768) = 30,720,000</code> queried dimensions and <code>(10,000 * 768) = 7,680,000</code> stored dimensions (within the included monthly allocation)</li>
<li>Separately, and excluding the included monthly allocation, this would be calculated as <code>(30,000 + 10,000) * 768 * ($0.01 / 1,000,000) + (10,000 * 768 * ($0.05 / 100,000,000))</code> and sum to $0.31 per month.</li>
</ul>
<div tabindex="-1" class="heading-wrapper level-h2"><h2 id="r2">R2</h2><a class="anchor-link" href="#r2"><span aria-hidden="true" class="anchor-icon"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path fill="currentcolor" d="m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z"></path></svg></span></a></div>
<p>R2 charges based on the total volume of data stored, along with two classes of operations on that data:</p>
<ol>
<li><strong>Class A operations</strong> which are more expensive and tend to mutate state.</li>
<li><strong>Class B operations</strong> which tend to read existing state.</li>
</ol>
<p>There are no charges for egress bandwidth.</p>

<aside role="note" aria-label="Containers documentation" class="aside-card flex items-start gap-3 rounded-lg px-4 py-3 my-4" style="--_c: var(--nb-info); --_t: var(--nb-info-muted);" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><span class="flex h-[1.375em] shrink-0 items-center" aria-hidden="true" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><svg width="1em" height="1em" viewBox="0 0 256 256" class="h-[1em] w-[1em]" data-astro-cid-znle5jil="true" data-icon="ph:info"><path fill="currentColor" d="M128 24a104 104 0 1 0 104 104A104.11 104.11 0 0 0 128 24m0 192a88 88 0 1 1 88-88a88.1 88.1 0 0 1-88 88m16-40a8 8 0 0 1-8 8a16 16 0 0 1-16-16v-40a8 8 0 0 1 0-16a16 16 0 0 1 16 16v40a8 8 0 0 1 8 8m-32-92a12 12 0 1 1 12 12a12 12 0 0 1-12-12"/></svg></span><div class="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col gap-0.5" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><p class="m-0 text-base leading-snug font-semibold" data-astro-cid-znle5jil>Containers documentation</p><div class="aside-card-body text-sm leading-normal" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><p>To learn more about Containers pricing, refer to <a href="/containers/platform/pricing/">Containers Pricing</a>.</p></div></div></aside>
<div tabindex="-1" class="heading-wrapper level-h2"><h2 id="service-bindings">Service bindings</h2><a class="anchor-link" href="#service-bindings"><span aria-hidden="true" class="anchor-icon"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path fill="currentcolor" d="m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z"></path></svg></span></a></div>
<p>Requests made from your Worker to another worker via a <a href="/workers/runtime-apis/bindings/service-bindings/">Service Binding</a> do not incur additional request fees. This allows you to split apart functionality into multiple Workers, without incurring additional costs.</p>
<p>For example, if Worker A makes a subrequest to Worker B via a Service Binding, or calls an RPC method provided by Worker B via a Service Binding, this is billed as:</p>
<ul>
<li>One request (for the initial invocation of Worker A)</li>
<li>The total amount of CPU time used across both Worker A and Worker B</li>
</ul>
<aside role="note" aria-label="Only available on Workers Standard pricing" class="aside-card flex items-start gap-3 rounded-lg px-4 py-3 my-4" style="--_c: var(--nb-info); --_t: var(--nb-info-muted);" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><span class="flex h-[1.375em] shrink-0 items-center" aria-hidden="true" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><svg width="1em" height="1em" viewBox="0 0 256 256" class="h-[1em] w-[1em]" data-astro-cid-znle5jil="true" data-icon="ph:info"><path fill="currentColor" d="M128 24a104 104 0 1 0 104 104A104.11 104.11 0 0 0 128 24m0 192a88 88 0 1 1 88-88a88.1 88.1 0 0 1-88 88m16-40a8 8 0 0 1-8 8a16 16 0 0 1-16-16v-40a8 8 0 0 1 0-16a16 16 0 0 1 16 16v40a8 8 0 0 1 8 8m-32-92a12 12 0 1 1 12 12a12 12 0 0 1-12-12"/></svg></span><div class="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col gap-0.5" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><p class="m-0 text-base leading-snug font-semibold" data-astro-cid-znle5jil>Only available on Workers Standard pricing</p><div class="aside-card-body text-sm leading-normal" data-astro-cid-znle5jil><p>If your Worker is on the deprecated Bundled or Unbound pricing plans, incoming requests from Service Bindings are charged the same as requests from the Internet. In the example above, you would be charged for two requests, one to Worker A, and one to Worker B.</p></div></div></aside>
<div tabindex="-1" class="heading-wrapper level-h2"><h2 id="fine-print">Fine Print</h2><a class="anchor-link" href="#fine-print"><span aria-hidden="true" class="anchor-icon"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path fill="currentcolor" d="m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z"></path></svg></span></a></div>
<p>Workers Paid plan is separate from any other Cloudflare plan (Free, Professional, Business) you may have. If you are an Enterprise customer, reach out to your account team to confirm pricing details.</p>
<p>Only requests that hit a Worker will count against your limits and your bill. Since Cloudflare Workers runs before the Cloudflare cache, the caching of a request still incurs costs. Refer to <a href="/workers/platform/limits/">Limits</a> to review definitions and behavior after a limit is hit.</p></article></div><nav aria-label="Pagination" class="border-border mt-12 flex items-center justify-between border-t pt-6"><a href="/workers/platform/" class="group text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground flex items-center gap-2 text-sm transition-colors"><svg width="1em" 11.32l-80-80a8 8 0 0 1 0-11.32l80-80a8 8 0 0 1 11.32 11.32L91.31 128Z"/></svg><span><span class="text-muted-foreground block text-xs">Previous</span><span class="text-foreground
